Method: UnitBoost：将生成式管理者替换为定义的元级算子。具体包括：1) 任务给定的单元映射将工人输出转化为槽位-值提案；2) 受限的argmax组装输出；3) 未填充或无支持的槽位形成显式残差，用于下一轮迭代。该过程顺序无关，记录单元溯源信息。

Result: 1) 在三个基准测试中，优于带黄金标签的最佳单一候选者0.060-0.195分，优于输入匹配的生成式管理者0.048-0.076分；2) 仅替换管理步骤即可提升六种复合系统配置的性能；3) 残差导向迭代显著提升FanOutQA细胞F1值（从0.4778至0.5524），证明真实残差优于随机目标和普通重读；4) 明确了三种无法获得增益的条件及跨单元耦合的修复成本。

Conclusion: 放弃语义自由的管理者换来了顺序不变性、单元溯源能力和可测试的失败条件。UnitBoost提供了一种更有序、可验证且高效的复合系统协调机制，证明了非生成式元级操作在某些场景下优于通用生成式LLM。

Abstract: Compound LLM systems often solve a coordination problem by adding a higher-level LLM. The resulting meta-agent reads workers' outputs, writes the final answer, allocates later calls, and decides when to stop. It is expressive, but it also concentrates three control decisions in an opaque, order-sensitive model call. We ask whether the manager needs to be generative at all. UnitBoost replaces that model with a defined meta-level operator: a task-given unit map turns worker outputs into slot-value proposals, a constrained argmax assembles the output, and the slots left unfilled or unsupported become an explicit residual for the next round. The operator is order-free, records unit provenance, and gives a simple guarantee: without coupling constraints, unit-wise maximization under the same admission score dominates selection of any complete candidate. On three held-out benchmarks, it exceeds the best single candidate chosen with gold labels by 0.060-0.195 absolute task-score points and input-matched generative managers by 0.048-0.076. Replacing only the management step improves six compound-system configurations by 0.013-0.182. Residual-directed rounds raise FanOutQA cell F1 from 0.4778 to 0.5524; matched controls show that the true residual outperforms random targets and ordinary rereading, while a label-free supply signal flags exhaustion after one unproductive round. The same analysis measures three conditions in which no such gain is available (one indivisible unit, unavailable unit identity, and an endpoint that charges for every emitted unit) and quantifies cross-unit coupling as a repair cost. The manager gives up semantic freedom and gains order invariance, unit provenance, and testable failure conditions.

</details>


### [179] [The Era by Eon Benchmark: A Generated Enterprise Estate with Exact Ground Truth for Benchmarking LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09853)
*Benjamin Gruenbaum,Doron Porat,Assaf Natanzon,Roy Zavida,Chen Dinachi,Or Itzahary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Era by Eon基准测试，通过构建包含完整虚构公司数据（产品模拟器、内部数据库等）的单一实体图，解决LLM代理在企业系统中因缺乏生产数据和真实标签而无法评估的问题，实现了精确评分和高逼真度验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的企业系统记录中，LLM代理无法在客户生产数据上进行评估，且没有现有的替代方案能提供“地面真值”（ground truth），导致评估困难。

Method: 1. 构建围绕完整虚构公司的基准测试，包括产品模拟器、特定内部数据库、基准问题和计算出的答案键。2. 使用种子定义的实体图提供共享公司数据给Salesforce、Zendesk等产品的模拟器。3. 利用问题条件生成器基于同一实体图生成内部数据库的模式和记录，确保企业环境的一致性。4. 所有预期答案均从最终记录中计算得出，实现精确评分。5. 通过设计/答案键检查验证内部数据库，通过真实性记分卡和对抗性检测器验证实体图。

Result: 在23个生成的公司中，平均真实性得分从61.8提升至97.0，且零条记录被标记为合成数据。在模拟器轨道对比中，9个模型回答33个问题各3次，准确率估计在42.4%到76.8%之间，校正后仍有3对模型差异得到支持。

Conclusion: 该基准测试通过一致的企业环境设计和精确的答案计算机制，有效解决了LLM代理在企业工具使用中的评估难题，并提供了高逼真度的模拟数据以支持可靠的模型比较。

Abstract: LLM agents for enterprise systems of record cannot be evaluated on customer production data, and no existing substitute provides ground truth. We present the Era by Eon Benchmark for evaluating LLM agents that use enterprise tools. The benchmark is built around a complete fictional company. It includes product simulators, company-specific internal databases, benchmark questions, and computed answer keys. Industry, company size, business model, application portfolio, and a seed define each company. One seeded entity graph supplies shared company data to simulators of Salesforce, Zendesk, Slack, Gong, and other products. A questionconditioned generator creates the schemas and records for internal databases. It takes shared entities, keys, and values from the same graph before generating database-specific facts. Both mechanisms therefore describe one consistent enterprise estate. Every expected answer is computed from the final records, so grading is exact. Design and answer-key checks validate the internal databases. A realism scorecard and adversarial detector validate the entity graph. Across 23 generated companies, the mean realism score rose from 61.8 to 97.0, with zero records flagged as synthetic. In the reported simulator-track comparison, nine models answered the same 33 questions three times each. Accuracy estimates ranged from 42.4% to 76.8%, and three of 36 pairwise differences remained supported after correction.

</details>


### [180] [Shifting Relational Paradigms for Affective Computing: Affective Resonance, Vitality Affects, and Vocal Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09864)
*Cy Gorman,Yihang Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于“互动场”的关系型情感计算框架，取代传统的个体状态范式，通过检测多模态对话中的方向性表达耦合来验证概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情感计算主要关注孤立说话者的离散情绪标签或唤醒度/效价，这种个体状态范式在处理人际互动时被认为是不完整的。

Method: 基于情感共振和活力轮廓理论，构建关系型分析框架；使用连续自监督语音表示进行实证研究，以检测多方对话中次秒级时间尺度的方向性表达耦合。

Result: 发现情感耦合具有机制特异性，集中在亚秒级时间尺度，且在排他性言语的负对照实验中消失，证实了情感动力学的关系性本质。

Conclusion: 确立了互动作为情感分析基本单位的地位，并提出了基于情感共振动态本体论的人工情感共振智能设计框架。

Abstract: Affective computing has largely followed an individual-state paradigm, extracting discrete emotion labels or arousal/valence from isolated speakers. We argue this framing is incomplete for interaction. Drawing on affective resonance and vitality-contour accounts, we propose a relational framework in which the primary unit of affective analysis is the interactional field constituted within vocal dynamics. As a proof of concept, we present a preliminary empirical study using continuous self-supervised speech representations to detect directional expressive coupling in multi-party conversation. Coupling is regime-specific, concentrated at sub-second timescales, and collapses under exclusive-speech negative controls, consistent with a relational account of affective dynamics. We introduce design frameworks for Artificial Affective Resonance Intelligence grounded in Affective Resonance Dynamic Ontologies, supported by null-calibrated directional coupling analyses across interaction regimes.

</details>


### [181] [AgentAudit: An Open, Extensible Framework for Full-Lifecycle Trust Evaluation of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09875)
*Shrey Nag,Sachita,Abhishek Kumar Singh,Lipi Goel,Rajeshwar Singh Janwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentAudit框架，通过评估完整执行轨迹来定位AI代理失败的具体阶段，揭示仅关注任务完成率的基准测试无法发现的信任度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架（如AgentBench, AgentDojo）仅评估AI代理的单一环节（如任务完成或安全性），无法覆盖规划、工具选择、执行、记忆和推理的完整流程，且难以精确定位故障来源。

Method: 构建AgentAudit框架，不替代也不干扰代理运行，而是读取记录的执行轨迹。从能力、落地、安全和行为四个维度（共10个子维度）进行评估，结合行为分类与故障归因，以 pinpoint 导致失败的确切阶段。对5个主流LLM在9项任务上进行评测。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5 (95.1) 和 GPT-5 (80.6) 综合信任得分最高；Sarvam 105B (57.6), Llama 3.3 70B (45.7) 和 Gemini 2.5 Flash (22.6) 显著落后。研究发现，任务完成率相似但信任度差异巨大，部分非前沿模型在对抗任务中表现为“不安全顺从”而非简单失败，这是传统通过/不通过基准测试无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: AgentAudit能全面评估LLM-based AI代理的信任度与可靠性，揭示了传统基准测试在识别深层安全行为和故障根源方面的局限性，为更细粒度的代理评估提供了新标准。

Abstract: Existing evaluation frameworks mostly assess only one part of AI agents, such as task completion (AgentBench) or security robustness (AgentDojo, ASB), rather than the complete pipeline of planning, tool selection, tool execution, memory and reasoning. Failures can occur at any stage, yet existing benchmarks rarely identify their precise source. AgentAudit evaluates the entire execution trace across ten capability, grounding, security and behavioural dimensions, namely instruction integrity, planner, memory, tool selection, tool invocation, tool correctness, alignment, tool faithfulness, security and execution integrity, combined with behavioural classification and failure attribution to pinpoint the exact stage responsible for an observed failure. AgentAudit can evaluate any LLM-based AI agent, since it attaches to the agent instead of replacing it. It reads only the recorded execution trace and does not interfere with how the agent runs, so it places no constraint on the agent's internal implementation. We evaluate five language models (OpenAI GPT-5, Claude Sonnet 5, Sarvam 105B, Llama 3.3 70B and Gemini 2.5 Flash) across nine capability and adversarial tasks. Claude Sonnet 5 and GPT-5 obtain the highest mean Composite Trust Scores (95.1 and 80.6 out of 100, respectively), while Sarvam 105B, Llama 3.3 70B and Gemini 2.5 Flash trail substantially (57.6, 45.7 and 22.6). All traces were scored by a single fixed judge model, which was itself one of the evaluated models, a limitation discussed in Section VII.E. More importantly, models with similar task-completion behaviour can diverge sharply in trustworthiness, as several non-frontier models are repeatedly classified Unsafe_Compliance on adversarial tasks rather than merely failing them, a distinction that pass/fail benchmarks cannot surface.

</details>


### [182] [Scored vs. Generated Readouts in Behavioral Language Models: An Empirical Study of Elicitation Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09882)
*Touchapon Kraisingkorn,Krittin Pachtrachai,Wachiravit Modecrua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对比了语言模型在零售任务中通过“评分答案令牌”与“生成理由后预测”两种读出的性能差异。结果显示，评分读出在13个单元格中的12个里准确率更高（AUC提升1.5-14.5点）。分析发现理由格式导致对主导特征依赖减少及收敛于库存表述。提出保留生成理由但使用评分头进行排序的策略以优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管微调后的语言模型能预测结果并生成解释，但在实际应用中，这两种输出通常被视为可互换的。然而，这种假设可能忽略了不同读出方式在准确性和校准度上的潜在差异，因此需要系统比较固定模型和提示下不同读出方法的性能。

Method: 在覆盖三个市场、四个零售任务的13个模型-领域单元中进行实验，包括两个使用完全公开数据和检查点的场景。通过固定模型检查点和提示内容，比较通过评分答案令牌获得的概率与在书面推理后生成的预测之间的差异。使用AUC、Brier分数等指标评估准确性、排序能力和校准度，并对约9,000条推理进行分析以识别相关因素。

Result: 1. 评分读出在12/13个单元格中排名更准确（p≈0.003），AUC提升1.5至14.5点。
2. 差距受任务特定监督和训练与服务格式不匹配的影响，从-2.2点到+13.7点不等。
3. 推理分析显示两个相关性：减少对主导预测特征的依赖，以及收敛于库存表述。
4. 第三种读出方法（在裁决前 eliciting 概率）改善了校准度（Brier分数从0.47降至0.15），但仅在训练数据中有代表性的结果率下有效；若评分头已校准，则其表现不如评分。

Conclusion: 不同读出方式对应不同的目标函数，导致性能差异。建议通过调整训练选择缩小差距，并提出一种混合策略：保留生成的推理以提供解释性，同时利用评分头获取更准确的排序结果，从而结合两者的优势。

Abstract: Language models fine-tuned on customer behavior can predict outcomes and generate explanations, but these readouts are often treated as interchangeable. Holding model checkpoint and prompt content fixed, we compare probabilities obtained by scoring answer tokens with predictions generated after a written rationale. Across 13 model-domain cells covering four retail tasks in three markets, including two using fully public data and checkpoints, the scored readout ranks outcomes more accurately in 12 of 13 cells (two-sided sign test, p approximately 0.003), by 1.5 to 14.5 points in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC). Paired bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero in every newly measured cell. The gap varies with task-specific supervision and mismatch between training and serving formats, ranging from -2.2 points for an untuned base model to +13.7 for rationale-format supervision. Analysis of approximately 9,000 rationales identifies two correlates: reduced reliance on the dominant predictive feature and convergence on stock formulations. Probability saturation does not track the gap. A third readout, eliciting a probability before any verdict, improves calibration (Brier score from 0.47 to 0.15) while ranking within noise of scoring, but only for outcome rates represented in training; it is worse than scoring when the scored head is already calibrated. We interpret these differences through the objectives matched by each readout, identify training choices that narrow the gap, and propose retaining generated rationales while sourcing ranking from the scored head.

</details>


### [183] [Decision Transformer for UAV-Mounted RIS-Assisted Dynamic D2D Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09885)
*Yaxuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无人机（UAV）搭载的可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的设备到设备（D2D）通信，通过联合优化UAV轨迹、姿态和RIS相位来最大化平均求和速率。研究采用深度强化学习和基于专家轨迹训练的决策Transformer来解决此问题，实现了有效的跨场景泛化，零样本迁移优于直接DRL迁移，在线微调以较少交互达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对UAV-RIS辅助D2D通信中存在的动态环境挑战，需要解决在移动性、能量和硬件约束下，如何有效联合优化UAV的运动参数与RIS反射特性以最大化通信速率的问题。

Method: 1. 建立包含UAV运动姿态、时变Rician角度及角度依赖RIS反射的复杂信道模型；
2. 构建联合优化UAV轨迹、姿态和RIS相位的数学问题，旨在最大化平均求和速率；
3. 提出基于深度强化学习（DRL）的解决方案，具体使用在多个场景专家轨迹上训练的决策Transformer（Decision Transformer）来处理序列决策问题。

Result: 1. 所提方法展现出有效的跨场景泛化能力；
2. 零样本迁移（Zero-shot transfer）性能优于直接的DRL迁移方法；
3. 在线微调（Online fine-tuning）在较少的交互次数下即可实现具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: 利用决策Transformer处理UAV-RIS辅助通信中的联合优化问题是可行的，该方法不仅提升了模型的泛化能力和迁移学习效率，还在资源受限（如交互次数少）的情况下保持了高性能，为动态无线通信网络的智能控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper studies unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-mouted reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted device-to-device (D2D) communication with stochastic link activation. It models UAV motion and attitude, time-varying Rician angles, and angle-dependent RIS reflection. A joint optimization of UAV trajectory, attitude, and RIS phases is formulated to maximize average sum rate under mobility, energy, and hardware constraints. The problem is addressed using deep reinforcement learning and a Decision Transformer trained on expert trajectories from multiple scenarios. Results demonstrate effective cross-scenario generalization, with zero-shot transfer outperforming direct DRL transfer and online fine-tuning achieving competitive performance with fewer interactions.

</details>


### [184] [Grounded Evaluation and Repair for NL-to-PDDL Problem Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09898)
*Joana Rosa,Pedro Santos,Valdemar Oliveira,Romão Silva,L. Miguel Silveira,Bruno Martins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出标准评估（语法正确性/规划成功率）会高估LLM生成PDDL问题的忠实度，提出结合LLM、验证器和迭代修复的端到端流水线，通过细粒度反馈提升质量。实验表明操作成功与基准参考重建存在显著差异，结构化修复有效，但PDDL 2.1的重建仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估标准（如语法有效性或规划器成功率）可能严重高估大语言模型（LLM）将自然语言转换为PDDL问题实例的忠实度；生成的问题虽可解析且可解，但可能错误表示初始状态、目标、对象结构或优化目标。

Method: 构建一个端到端的NL-to-PDDL流水线，结合LLM生成、PDDL解析检查、规划与验证、领域一致性检查器、LLM批评者及迭代修复机制。利用领域描述、生成问题、自然语言描述和操作诊断信息构建细粒度修复反馈，并采用基于参考的基准分析进行后验评估。

Result: 在Planetarium、AutoPlanBench和PDDL~2.1问题上，发现操作成功率和基准参考重建结果之间存在显著分歧。结构化修复被证明是有益的，但在PDDL~2.1上即使操作成功率提高，参考重建仍然困难。

Conclusion: 仅依赖操作成功不足以衡量LLM生成PDDL的质量，需引入更严格的语义和结构一致性检查；虽然迭代修复能提升性能，但处理复杂PDDL版本（如2.1）时仍面临重大挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for translating Natural Language (NL) planning descriptions into PDDL problem instances. However, standard evaluation criteria such as syntactic validity or planner success can substantially overestimate faithfulness to the described task: a generated problem may be parseable and solvable while misrepresenting the intended initial state, goal, object structure, or optimization target. This paper studies an end-to-end NL-to-PDDL pipeline that combines LLM generation, checks in terms of PDDL parsing, planning and validation, a domain-conformance checker, an LLM critic, and iterative repair. Fine-grained repair feedback is constructed from the domain description, the generated problem, the natural language problem description, and operational diagnostics. Reference-based comparisons against curated benchmark PDDL problem descriptions are used for post-hoc benchmark analysis, and these offline checks include renaming-invariant structural matching and semantic equivalence, where domain support is available. Across Planetarium, AutoPlanBench, and curated PDDL~2.1 problems, results show that operational success and benchmark-reference reconstruction can diverge substantially. Results also show that structured repair can be useful, and that PDDL~2.1 remains challenging for reference reconstruction, even when operational success improves.

</details>


### [185] [Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention for Chunked Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09925)
*Shengye Dong,Haochen Niu,Hao Liu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Shanmin Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TFGCA模块，通过时频分解和几何注意力机制解决VLA模型动作预测中的频率纠缠和正交相位关系捕捉不足问题，显著提升分布内及分布外任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略将动作块视为通用隐藏token序列，导致无法有效处理：1）不同时间尺度上的平滑趋势与精细修正运动的频率混合；2）不同运动阶段（如抓取、调整）在表示空间中接近正交但任务相关的几何关系，因为点积注意力对正交方向不敏感。

Method: 引入时间-频率几何交叉注意力（TFGCA）模块：使用可学习的平稳小波变换将动作块分解为时频token；通过交叉注意力融合点积（相似度）和楔积模长（对近正交性敏感），以学习权重结合两者；采用零初始化残差连接确保初始行为与基线一致，支持直接叠加到预训练VLA上进行联合微调。

Result: 相比同源基线模型，TFGCA在LIBERO基准上平均提升+1.5，OOD LIBERO-Plus提升+6.3，RoboTwin域随机化下平均提升+28.5，AgiBot A2真实机器人任务整体成功率提升+11.67个百分点，且在分布外场景增益更显著。

Conclusion: TFGCA通过显式建模动作的时频结构和几何正交关系，有效弥补了传统VLA模型的表征盲点，大幅提升了机器人在复杂和泛化场景下的动作执行能力。

Abstract: Modern vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict a whole chunk of actions: one to two seconds of coordinated motion emitted in a single forward pass. Yet an action chunk is essentially a short multivariate trajectory, but inside these models it is a sequence of generic per-timestep hidden tokens decoded by a linear head. This under-serves two motion structures. First, frequency: a chunk superimposes a smooth global trend and fine corrective motion across time scales, and a single token entangles them. Second, cross-phase geometry: motions of different phases (reach, contact, grasp adjustment, settling) unfold along very different, near-orthogonal directions in representation space, yet are tightly related for the task and arise across the time axis. Dot-product attention scores alignment by an inner product, so it favors aligned tokens and is least sensitive near orthogonality, leaving such relationships for the network to recover through a detour.
  We introduce Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention (TFGCA), a drop-in module repairing both blind spots. TFGCA uses a per-dimension learnable stationary wavelet transform to decompose the action chunk into time-frequency tokens, and each time token retrieves information from them via a cross-attention that fuses the dot product (similarity) with the wedge-product magnitude (sensitive to near-orthogonality) through a learnable weight. A zero-initialized residual reproduces the base behavior at initialization, so it can be dropped onto a pretrained VLA and fine-tuned jointly. Relative to the same-source base, TFGCA improves in-distribution LIBERO by +1.5 on average, the OOD LIBERO-Plus by +6.3, the randomized average under RoboTwin domain randomization by +28.5, and the overall success rate on three real-robot AgiBot A2 tasks by +11.67 points, with larger gains out of distribution.

</details>


### [186] [Structural Process Supervision for Latent Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09928)
*Yiqi Li,Xu Chen,Chen Ju,Jiangchao Yao,Zhaoyang Li,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出原型中介过程监督（PMPS），通过可学习推理原型提供结构化过程级监督，解决潜在推理中的表示坍缩问题，显著压缩输出长度并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法缺乏对潜在嵌入的直接过程监督，导致表示坍缩和信息分布不均。

Method: 引入可学习推理原型作为语义锚点，将潜在嵌入和显式思维链（CoT）嵌入投影到共享原型空间，实现多对多软对齐；结合渐进式序列对齐（PSA）模块引导训练。

Result: 在GSM8K-Aug上将输出token长度压缩至显式CoT的50%以下；相比SIM-CoT基线，平均准确率提升2.08%；在小模型上超越CoT-SFT，在大模型及挑战性任务中保持最高准确率。

Conclusion: PMPS有效解决了潜在推理的监督缺失问题，实现了高效、鲁棒且高精度的推理性能。

Abstract: Latent reasoning approaches enhance token-level efficiency and robustness by replacing verbose, explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens with compact continuous-space embeddings. However, existing methods lack direct process supervision over these latent embeddings, which often leads to representation collapse and uneven information distribution. To address this, we propose Prototype-Mediated Process Supervision (PMPS), which introduces learnable reasoning prototypes as semantic anchors to provide structural process-level supervision for latent reasoning. PMPS projects latent embeddings and explicit CoT embeddings into a shared prototype space, achieving many-to-many soft alignment between unequal-length representations through prototype assignment. Meanwhile, we introduce a Progressive Sequential Alignment (PSA) module to further guide training: positional priors initially encourage sequential alignment structure, then gradually relax to permit adaptive matching. Experimental results show that PMPS compresses output token length to under 50% of explicit CoT on GSM8K-Aug. Compared to leading baseline SIM-CoT, our method achieves average accuracy gains of 2.08% across different model families. On GPT-2, PMPS even surpasses CoT-SFT. On larger models and a more challenging task, PMPS consistently attains the highest accuracy among all latent reasoning methods with comparable output length.

</details>


### [187] [Belief-State Engine: Augmenting LLMs for Principled Planning Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10036)
*Arnab Chattopadhayay,Debdipta Halder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Belief-State Engine (BSE)架构，通过在LLM外部维护贝叶斯后验信念状态，解决大语言模型在部分可观测环境中的决策缺陷，显著提升任务回报与信念校准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）代理在部分可观测环境中表现不佳，表现为模糊反馈导致过早承诺、单一观察引发错误假设、以及随历史增长策略漂移。根本原因是常规部署的LLM代理缺乏对隐藏状态的显式信念建模，仅为历史条件策略。

Method: 提出“信念状态引擎”（BSE），作为置于LLM外部的推理模块。BSE在给定POMDP模型的潜在状态上维护贝叶斯后验分布，并在每个决策步骤仅向LLM暴露该后验分布而非原始动作-观察日志。通过四条公理规范信念一致性内部状态，证明LLM与BSE组合构成信念MDP上的马尔可夫策略，从而继承经典POMDP理论的贝尔曼最优性保证。

Result: 在Tiger POMDP和红队攻击图任务中，BSE增强型代理相比六种基线（反应式LLM、CoT、ReAct、自然语言信念跟踪器、QMDP、POMCP）在任务回报、信念校准和决策一致性上均有提升。十项针对性消融实验证实了各架构组件的贡献，且效果不依赖于特定模型。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯信念状态管理与LLM解耦是解决部分可观测环境下LLM代理结构性缺陷的有效方法。该方法不仅提升了性能，还提供了理论上的最优性保证，为构建更鲁棒的AI代理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents produce fluent action sequences across a wide range of tasks, yet they fail in characteristic ways once the environment becomes partially observable. Ambiguous feedback pushes them into premature commitments. A single informative observation can collapse their uncertainty onto the wrong hypothesis. Policies drift as the history grows. We trace these symptoms to a common structural cause. An LLM agent, as commonly deployed, is a history-conditioned policy with no explicit belief over hidden state.
  We propose an architectural fix. The Belief-State Engine (BSE) is an inference module placed outside the LLM. It maintains a Bayesian posterior over the latent states of a given POMDP (Partially Observable Markov Decision Process) model, and at each decision step it exposes only that posterior to the LLM. The raw action-observation log is not shown. We set out a minimal four-axiom specification of what a belief-consistent internal state must satisfy, and prove that the LLM paired with the BSE is a sound Markov policy on the belief MDP induced by the underlying POMDP. It therefore inherits the Bellman optimality guarantees of classical POMDP theory, provided the LLM is never exposed to the raw history.
  We evaluate the architecture on the Tiger POMDP and a red-team attack-graph task, against six baselines: a reactive LLM, Chain-of-Thought, ReAct, a natural-language belief tracker, QMDP, and POMCP. Across both domains, the BSE-augmented agent improves task return, belief calibration, and decision consistency. Ten targeted ablations isolate the contribution of each architectural choice confirms that the effect is not specific to any one model. Code, environment specifications, prompt templates, and seed logs accompany this paper.

</details>


### [188] [OntologyAligner: Ontology-Aligned Retrieval and Hierarchy-Guided Large Language Model Reranking for Biomedical Ontology Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10055)
*Jie Song,Zhichuan Xu,Ziyu Lu,Meng Xiao,Cheng Bi,Yuxin Zhang,Xin Zheng,Xiaoran Li,Qiongfang Cao,Hao Yang,Bairong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OntologyAligner框架，通过检索、LLM重排序和层级细化三阶段流程，解决生物医学本体标准化难题，并在HPO基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学术语存在词汇变异及层级概念细微差异，导致自由文本到标准化概念的映射困难，阻碍数据整合与分析。

Method: 构建三阶段框架：1. 本体对齐检索；2. 大语言模型候选重排序；3. 选择性层级引导细化。同时构建PhenoNormBench基准（含7个HPO数据集共13,390样本）。

Result: 在HPO标准化任务中达到88.78% Macro Top-1准确率和86.75% Micro Top-1准确率，分别超越最强基线4.85和5.07个百分点。消融实验验证各阶段互补性，跨本体（MONDO等）应用证明其可移植性。

Conclusion: OntologyAligner提供了一个通用且可泛化的框架，能准确将生物医学文本映射至结构化本体概念，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Biomedical ontology normalization maps free-text expressions to standardized concepts, enabling consistent integration and analysis of biomedical data. This task remains challenging because lexical variation and subtle distinctions among hierarchically related concepts can obscure concept boundaries. We present OntologyAligner, a three-stage framework that combines ontology-aligned retrieval, large language model candidate reranking, and selective hierarchy-guided refinement. We also construct PhenoNormBench, a unified benchmark comprising 13,390 samples from seven Human Phenotype Ontology datasets. OntologyAligner achieved state-of-the-art performance on HPO normalization, with 88.78% Macro Top-1 Accuracy and 86.75% Micro Top-1 Accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.85 and 5.07 percentage points, respectively. Ablation analyses showed complementary contributions from all three stages, and sensitivity analyses demonstrated stability across candidate-set sizes and model backbones. Applications to MONDO, MEDIC, and NCBITaxon further established portability to other ontologies. OntologyAligner offers a generalizable framework for accurate mapping of biomedical text to structured ontology concepts. PhenoNormBench and the code are publicly available at https://github.com/zhelishisongjie/OntologyAligner.

</details>


### [189] [Reference-Based Bias Detection in LLMs via Relative Representations of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10060)
*Marek Jeliński,Jan Dubiński,Maciej Chrabaszcz,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于参考的隐藏状态偏差审计方法，通过计算表示偏差偏移量（ΔB），在无需任务特定数据的情况下，以极低的计算成本快速检测模型微调过程中的内部偏见变化，且与输出级偏见高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏见审计方法主要依赖模型输出，不仅成本高（需要基准测试或裁判模型），而且可能遗漏那些未体现在生成文本中的内部表示变化。

Method: 提出了一种基于锚点句子的相对表示方法。由于微调改变了表示几何结构，直接比较绝对隐藏状态不可行，因此通过将句子编码为相对于固定锚点集合的相似度，构建共享比较空间。在此空间中测量目标群体与正负属性关联的变化，定义为“表示偏差偏移量”（Representational Bias Shift, ΔB）。

Result: 在三个模型家族及多个基准测试中，ΔB 与输出级偏见变化在15/18的设置下显著相关（最高|r|=0.84）。通过阈值化ΔB可检测偏见增加的检查点，其ROC AUC介于0.65至0.99之间，优于SEAT基线。该方法对锚点、属性和模板变化稳定，无需任务特定数据，审计速度约3分钟，计算量仅为现有输出级基准的1/3至1/50。

Conclusion: 该方法是输出级审计的有效补充而非替代，能够高效、低成本地监控模型内部偏见的动态变化，特别是在参数高效适应场景下具有独特的诊断价值。

Abstract: Existing bias auditing methods typically rely on model outputs, requiring costly benchmarks or judge models and potentially missing internal shifts that never appear in generated text. We propose a reference-based method that audits bias in hidden-state representations across related model variants, for example before and after fine-tuning. Because fine-tuning reshapes representation geometry, absolute hidden states are not directly comparable, so we encode each sentence by its similarities to a fixed set of anchor sentences, yielding relative representations in a shared comparison space. There we measure how target groups shift in their association with positive and negative attributes, a quantity we call the Representational Bias Shift $ΔB$. Across three model families and the WildGuardMix, DecodingTrust and ToxiGen benchmarks, $ΔB$ correlates with output-level bias change in 15 of the 18 settings we test, reaching $|r| = 0.84$ ($p < 0.001$) under full fine-tuning and becoming more model-dependent under parameter-efficient adaptation. Thresholding $ΔB$ detects checkpoints whose bias increased with ROC AUC between $0.65$ and $0.99$, and on WildGuardMix and DecodingTrust it separates them better than a SEAT-based baseline for all three families. $ΔB$ is also stable under changes to the anchor set, attribute sets and target templates. Our method requires no task-specific evaluation data and audits a model in about three minutes, using $3$-$50\times$ less compute than the output-level benchmarks considered here. We view it as complementary to output-based auditing rather than a replacement for it.

</details>


### [190] [RAP: Research Attention Prediction Reveals Target-Conditioned Evidence Acquisition Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10092)
*Yingqian Wu,Jingcong Liang,Siyuan Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Philip Torr,Junchi Yu,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Research Attention Prediction (RAP)基准评估LLM追踪研究热点变化的能力，发现尽管搜索有帮助，但模型在预测精度上仍不及简单的统计基线（EWMA），主要瓶颈在于对近期证据的检索不足及未来更新的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）日益作为研究代理，但由于综述和研究思想缺乏唯一可验证的结果，难以评估其追踪研究注意力转移的能力。

Method: 引入滚动基准RAP，涵盖278个AI/ML领域和1,390个片段。LLM代理在时间受限的arXiv语料库中进行搜索，并预测未来六个月八个固定研究方向的论文占比。对比不同诊断模型与指数加权移动平均（EWMA）基线的表现，分析历史访问限制、证据回放及微调策略的影响。

Result: 搜索通常有助于提升性能，但所有四种诊断模型的组合准确性均低于精确计数的EWMA基线。发现两个关联瓶颈：1. 在累积历史访问下，状态保持优于直接预测；2. 冻结证据回放显示，面向预测的策略检索到的近期证据较少。即使拥有精确历史活动，特定于未来的更新依然有限，仅GPT-5.5配合重新开启搜索略超EWMA。在实现结果上微调Qwen3-4B提升了预测Spearman相关性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在追踪研究注意力变化方面存在显著局限，主要受限于对近期关键证据的检索效率及对未来动态的更新能力，简单的统计方法在某些场景下更具优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as research agents, yet their ability to track shifts in research attention is difficult to evaluate because reviews and research ideas lack uniquely verifiable outcomes. We introduce Research Attention Prediction (RAP), a rolling benchmark covering 278 AI/ML fields and 1,390 episodes. At each cut-off, an LLM agent searches a temporally restricted arXiv corpus and predicts the next six months' paper shares across eight frozen research directions. Search generally helps, but all four diagnostic models perform worse than an exact-count exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) baseline in compositional accuracy. We identify two linked bottlenecks. Under cumulative-history access, State carry-forward outperforms direct Forecast for all four diagnostic models; frozen-evidence replay links a shared component of this reversal to Forecast-oriented policies retrieving a smaller share of recent evidence. Even with exact historical activity, future-specific updating remains limited, with only GPT-5.5 plus reopened Search slightly surpassing EWMA. Fine-tuning on realised outcomes improves Qwen3-4B's forecast Spearman correlation by 0.105 on held-out fields at later origins, with gains also on change-rich episodes.

</details>


### [191] [Agent-Based ML-LLM Fusion with Self-Optimizing Prompts for Plateau Weather Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10135)
*Shuai Yan,Yang Xu,Shan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SmartWeatherAgent，一种融合LLM与LightGBM的三阶段旅游气象服务架构，通过意图识别、灾害预测和推理增强生成，显著提升了预警质量（S_final从4.2升至8.9）及科学严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有旅游气象服务中上下文理解不足、泛化能力弱及场景适应性差的问题。

Method: 构建统一三阶段架构：1) 结合规则与大模型进行多粒度意图解析；2) 使用含高原特征（如风速突变率）的LightGBM进行灾害预测（F1-Macro 0.605, 延迟1.60ms）；3) 实施12轮微步提示自优化循环以增强生成质量。

Result: 综合预警质量评分S_final提升112%（从4.2至8.9）；数据引用(B08)从6.5升至8.5；科学严谨性达峰值9.2；系统能自主生成包含因果机制、时空演化及定量证据的结构化预警。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了从被动响应向主动感知、可解释决策及智能代理的转变，增强了气象服务的专业深度、逻辑严谨性与科学性。

Abstract: To address insufficient contextualization, weak generalization, and poor scenario adaptation in tourism meteorological services, we propose SmartWeatherAgent--a unified three-stage architecture integrating intent recognition, hazard prediction, and reasoning-enhanced generation. The system fuses rule-based methods with large language models to parse queries at multiple granularities and employs a LightGBM model enriched with highland-specific features (e.g., wind speed abruptness rate), achieving an F1-Macro score of 0.605 with 1.60 ms latency on high-wind, precipitation, and low-temperature events. A 12-round micro-step prompt self-optimization loop boosts the composite warning quality score S_final from 4.2 (B01) to 8.9 (B12, +112%). Key improvements include a sharp rise in B08 from data source citation (6.5 -> 8.5), sustained high performance in B10 via physical mechanism explanation, and a peak scientific rigor score of 9.2 in B12 through explicit uncertainty statements. The system autonomously generates structured warnings that integrate causal mechanisms, spatiotemporal evolution, quantitative evidence, regulatory references, and confidence statements--enhancing professional depth, logical rigor, and scientific soundness, and advancing meteorological services toward proactive perception, explainable decision-making, and intelligent agency.

</details>


### [192] [Kernel-Managed Shared Memory for System-Wide Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10144)
*Ryan Lum,Yongfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出内核管理的共享内存机制，通过集中式内核管理检索和隐私，显著提升AI代理个性化效果，同时大幅降低延迟和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，单个代理学到的有用上下文往往无法被其他代理获取，且由代理自行管理检索和隐私存在局限性。

Method: 设计并实现了一种系统级抽象“内核管理的共享内存”，由代理写入结构化、带标签的记忆，由内核统一控制检索、隐私执行和提示注入。在AIOS上针对GPT-4o、Llama-3.1:8B、Qwen-2.5:7B三个模型进行了1,800次试验评估，并与Mem0、标准RAG注入及全量上下文拼接三种方案对比。

Result: 相比未管理的Mem0后端，内核管理的检索和注入使个性化评分提升2.4-4.0分（p < 10^-18）；相比标准RAG注入也有显著且一致的增益；相比全量上下文拼接，性能相当或略低，但提示长度大幅缩短，端到端延迟降低15-61%，Token使用量和推理成本相应减少。

Conclusion: 将记忆管理集中在代理系统内核中，而非交由个体代理处理，能在以极低成本获得几乎等同于无限制上下文的个性化收益。

Abstract: AI systems become more useful when they can adapt to the people using them, but in multi-agent systems, useful context learned by one agent often remains unavailable to others. We present kernel-managed shared memory, a system-level abstraction in which specialized agents write structured, tagged memories while the agent-system kernel, not individual agents, governs retrieval, privacy enforcement, and prompt injection. We implement and evaluate this design on AIOS and compare it against three alternatives across three assistant models (GPT-4o, Llama-3.1:8B, Qwen-2.5:7B) and 1,800 total trials. Against an unmanaged external memory backend (Mem0) using identical underlying storage, kernel-managed retrieval and injection improve personalization scores by 2.4-4.0 points on a 5-point scale (e.g., 1.05 to 4.69 profile usage on GPT-4o), with every comparison significant at p < 10^-18. Against standard retrieval-augmented injection, gains are similarly large and consistent across all three models. Against full, unfiltered context concatenation, a soft ceiling on available context rather than on response quality, kernel-managed injection statistically matches performance on two of three models and shows a small, model-specific deficit on the third, while using substantially shorter prompts: end-to-end latency is 15-61% lower across all three models, with corresponding reductions in per-call token usage and inference cost. These results indicate that centralizing memory management in the agent-system kernel, rather than leaving retrieval and privacy enforcement to individual agents, delivers most of the personalization benefit of unconstrained context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond Surface Imitation: Contrastive Modeling for Reasoning Path Alignment in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10177)
*Mingbo Yang,Wenqiang Wang,Zhaolu Kang,Peng Chen,Yannan Chen,Sunshang Wang,Yan Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合对比演示建模与自优化能力的多模态上下文学习（ICL）框架，通过显式对比次优/优响应及推理路径，并引入响应条件检索机制，显著提升MLLM在复杂任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表面模仿的多模态ICL方法难以使模型对齐输入所需的推理路径，尤其在复杂任务中限制了性能提升。

Method: 1. 构建对比演示：对同一输入提供次优与优响应，并附带揭示优化过程的推理路径；2. 响应条件检索：根据当前响应选择相关推理路径的演示；3. 轻量级对齐控制器：预测响应质量以决定是否需要进一步细化。

Result: 在三种类型的多模态任务上实验表明，该框架持续提升了多模态大语言模型的性能，其中在视觉问答（VQA）任务上的提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式化推理路径和动态检索机制，有效克服了表面模仿的局限，为多模态ICL提供了更有效的改进方向。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is widely used in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and achieves strong performance across a wide range of multimodal tasks. However, existing multimodal ICL methods often rely on surface level imitation of in-context demonstrations, making it difficult for MLLMs to align their responses with the reasoning path required by the given multimodal input. This limitation becomes more pronounced in complex multimodal tasks, thereby restricting further improvements in MLLM performance. To address this issue, we propose a new multimodal ICL framework that combines contrastive demonstration modeling with the self-refinement capability of MLLMs. Specifically, our framework reformulates each demonstration by explicitly contrasting a suboptimal response with a better response under the same input, together with a reasoning path that reveals how the response should be refined. This contrastive formulation makes the reasoning path toward the desired response more explicit and guides the MLLM beyond superficial imitation. Furthermore, because effective refinement depends on the current response, we introduce a response-conditioned retrieval mechanism to select demonstrations whose reasoning paths are more relevant to the current response. In addition, we use a lightweight alignment controller to predict response quality and determine whether further refinement is needed. Experiments on three types of multimodal tasks show that the proposed framework consistently improves MLLM performance, with particularly notable gains on visual question answering (VQA).

</details>


### [194] [Why Sample What You Can Enumerate? Exact Policy Optimization for Genomic Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10221)
*Haoyue Liu,Xiaoyu Ma,Ye Chen,Zhichao Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对在可枚举工具子集空间中GRPO因采样碰撞导致优势消失的问题，提出FGPO方法。通过全量评分和预计算奖励表，实现精确动作期望优化并移除训练中的推理器调用，显著提升基因组推理性能并降低工具调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家科学领域（如基因组推理），工具子集空间虽小且可枚举，但传统的基于冻结推理器的强化学习配方（如GRPO）存在结构性错配。随着策略集中，重采样导致奖励碰撞，组归一化优势消失，甚至出现无奖励信号比例大幅上升的问题。

Method: 提出完整组策略优化（FGPO）。该方法包含两个核心机制：(1) 对所有工具子集进行评分，优化精确的动作期望，确保每次更新覆盖完整的动作空间；(2) 预先计算每个问题-子集对的奖励并存入穷举表，从而完全从训练循环中移除对冻结推理器的调用。

Result: 在五个冻结推理器和三个基因组基准测试的15种设置下，FGPO均优于GRPO，平均提升6.75分，最高提升14.20分。相比之下，标准按需GRPO需要2.4倍的推理器评估次数。此外，在GenomeQA上，FGPO将每道题目的平均工具调用次数从2.36降至1.40。

Conclusion: FGPO有效解决了小样本、可枚举工具空间下的强化学习估计退化问题，不仅提高了模型性能，还显著减少了计算资源和推理器调用的开销，为特定领域的工具使用策略学习提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning over a frozen reasoner has become a common recipe for teaching a policy which external tools to invoke. We show that this recipe becomes structurally mismatched in specialist scientific settings where the complete tool-subset space is enumerable. There, a small set of recurring computational capabilities covers the domain, so the space of tool subsets is combinatorial yet small enough to enumerate, and GRPO still estimates an action expectation from a handful of sampled rollouts. Worse, the approximation degrades as training succeeds: as the policy concentrates on preferred subsets it resamples them, sampled rewards collide, and the group-normalized advantage vanishes. On genomic reasoning the fraction of questions yielding no reward signal rises from 0.2% under a uniform reference policy to 20.8% after GRPO training. As a remedy, we introduce FGPO (Full-Group Policy Optimization), which (1) scores every tool subset and optimizes the exact action expectation, so each update sees the complete action space, and (2) precomputes the reward of each question--subset pair into an exhaustive table, removing frozen-reasoner calls from the training loop entirely. Across five frozen reasoners and three genomic benchmarks, FGPO outperforms GRPO in all 15 settings by 6.75 points on average and up to 14.20, while a standard on-demand GRPO schedule would require 2.4 times as many frozen-reasoner reward evaluations and, on GenomeQA, FGPO cuts invoked tools per question from 2.36 to 1.40.

</details>


### [195] [What Should an Agent Forget? Separating What Is Stored from What Is Used](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10263)
*Yuhang Li,Yuchen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RD-Forget框架，通过分离存储与使用机制，解决语言代理中过时事实误导当前回答的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化语言代理需要存储经验以跨越时间保持可用，但每个回答都需要针对特定问题的证据。过时的错误事实可能误导当前状态的回答，但对于历史查询仍然重要。

Method: 构建无训练（training-free）的RD-Forget框架：1) 保留源档案存储观察结果；2) 基于查询条件的记忆视图控制其对当前回答的影响；3) 冻结的语言模型策展人提取相关证据、将事实分组为语义槽并保留多跳推理所需的关系；4) 同槽替换链接抑制当前语境中的过时值，意图感知检索使早期证据重新可用；5) 率失真公式指导在内存预算内构建回答时视图。

Result: 在对话记忆、知识更新、事实整合、长上下文推理和个性化等实验中，结果表明准确答案与查询相关证据的构建及对过时替代方案的控制密切相关。缺乏遗忘或查询调节的配置得分损失最大，而语义槽分组、历史访问和关系保留功能互补。

Conclusion: 保留历史的同时选择性控制其使用，提供了一种适应变化事实和应对未来问题的实用方法。

Abstract: Persistent language agents need stored experience to remain available across time, while each answer requires evidence suited to a particular question. A superseded fact can mislead a current-state answer and still be essential for a historical query. We present RD-Forget, a training-free framework that separates what an agent stores from what it uses. A retained source archive preserves observations, and a query-conditioned memory view controls their influence on the current answer. A frozen language-model curator extracts relevant evidence, groups facts into semantic slots, and preserves the relations needed for multi-hop reasoning. Same-slot replacement links suppress superseded values in current-state contexts, while intent-aware retrieval makes earlier evidence eligible again. A rate-distortion formulation guides construction of the answer-time view within a memory budget. Experiments span conversational memory, knowledge updating, fact consolidation, long-context reasoning, and personalization under a shared answering pipeline. The results associate accurate answers with both query-relevant evidence construction and control over obsolete alternatives. Configurations without forgetting or query conditioning have the largest score deficits, while slot grouping, historical access, and relation preservation contribute complementary functions. Retaining history while selectively controlling its use offers a practical way to accommodate changing facts and future questions.

</details>


### [196] [TRACE: Training Reasoning Agents for Causal Exploration with Synthesized Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10315)
*Rui Sun,Zhan Shi,Bing He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE环境，通过模拟器生成干预数据以提供可验证奖励，解决复杂诊断推理中缺乏客观反馈的问题。RL训练显著提升Qwen3.5模型性能，证明可扩展的客观训练信号比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在数学和代码等领域成功，因为这些领域的答案易于验证。然而，复杂数据的诊断推理（如确定异常原因）通常缺乏这种优势，因为建立真实原因往往需要昂贵的专家调查且事后可能模糊不清。研究旨在工程化这种验证不对称性，使此类任务也能受益于强化学习。

Method: 构建TRACE数字广告诊断环境，包含12个根本原因和细粒度细分归因。通过采样干预并注入受控模拟器来生成观测数据，从而获得隐藏干预作为“神谕”标签和客观奖励。智能体使用Python和SQL调查噪声、混淆及分布式的证据，识别根本原因及受影响的分段分配。采用监督微调（SFT）结合合成奖励的强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在235个测试集上：最强提示基线Claude Opus 5得分为0.686；Qwen3.5-35B-A3B经SFT提升至0.637，经RL训练后达到0.757，超越所有评估的提示基线（包括前沿闭源模型和更大的Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）。最终策略使用的工具调用次数显著少于提示式35B基础模型。

Conclusion: 获取可扩展的客观训练信号是比模型规模更关键的限制因素。基于模拟器的验证方法可以使原本模糊的诊断推理任务变得适合可扩展的强化学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has advanced language-model reasoning in domains such as mathematics and code, where objective answers are inexpensive to check. Diagnostic reasoning over complex data lacks this advantage: establishing the true cause of an anomaly often requires costly expert investigation and may remain ambiguous after the fact. We ask whether this asymmetry of verification can instead be engineered. We sample an intervention, inject it into a controlled simulator, and generate the observations it would produce. The hidden intervention provides an oracle label and objective reward, while the agent must still investigate noisy, confounded, and distributed evidence.
  We instantiate this approach in TRACE, a digital-advertising diagnostic environment with 12 root causes and fine-grained segment attribution. Agents investigate each episode using Python and SQL and must identify both the root cause and, when applicable, the affected segment assignment. On a held-out 235-episode test set, the strongest prompted baseline, Claude Opus 5, reaches 0.686 FullAttr@1. Supervised fine-tuning raises Qwen3.5-35B-A3B from 0.159 to 0.637, and subsequent RL with synthesized rewards reaches 0.757, outperforming all evaluated prompted baselines, including frontier closed-source models and a prompted Qwen3.5-122B-A10B model. The resulting policy also uses substantially fewer tool calls than the prompted 35B base. These results provide evidence that access to a scalable, objective training signal can be a more important constraint than model scale alone. More broadly, simulation-based verification can make otherwise ambiguous diagnostic reasoning tasks amenable to scalable reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [197] [From Symbolic Perception to Logical Deduction: A Framework for Guiding Language Models in Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10335)
*Weichen Dai,Rafael Medeiros Cabral,Ziyi Shou,Yan Cao,Xin Shen,Dongcai Lu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合几何视觉解析器和符号求解器的纯LLM框架，在2025年中考几何题基准测试中性能媲美Gemini 2.5 Pro，且推理过程更透明、可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI在平面几何任务中视觉感知与数学推理整合的难题；克服大型多模态模型（LMMs）计算成本高和“黑盒”不透明的缺陷；提升大语言模型（LLMs）处理复杂几何问题的能力。

Method: 构建一个包含“几何视觉解析器”（将图形转化为符号形式）和“符号求解器”（执行形式化演绎）的框架；使用2025年中国中考真题构建新颖的挑战性基准进行测试。

Result: 该方法在复杂几何问题上达到了与Gemini 2.5 Pro相当的性能水平，同时提供了更清晰、类似人类的解决方案，有效减少了幻觉并增强了推理的可解释性。

Conclusion: 纯LLM通过集成专用模块即可在平面几何任务中匹敌最先进的多模态模型，证明了符号化推理在处理视觉-语言混合任务中的有效性及优越的可解释性。

Abstract: Plane geometry remains a significant challenge in AI, requiring the integration of visual perception and mathematical reasoning. While Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) naturally handle visuo-linguistic inputs, they are often computationally intensive and opaque. We demonstrate that a pure Large Language Model (LLM), when equipped with specialized modules, can rival state-of-the-art LMMs on complex geometry problems. Our framework integrates a Geometric Vision Parser, which translates diagrams into symbolic form, with a Symbolic Solver that performs formal deductions, thereby mitigating hallucinations and promoting interpretable reasoning. To enable rigorous evaluation, we curate a benchmark of challenging problems from the 2025 Chinese Zhongkao examinations, ensuring data novelty and testing deeper deductive skills. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves performance comparable to Gemini 2.5 Pro while delivering clearer, human-like solutions.

</details>


### [198] [Cyber-Financial Contagion: Modeling the Propagation of an AI Vendor Compromise Through the Banking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10350)
*Alex Leytes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI供应商集中化带来的金融风险，提出CFC-Prop模型模拟黑客攻击引发的连锁反应，并开发CFC-GNN早期预警模型，证明AI供应商网络安全是金融稳定性的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行系统高度依赖少数共享AI供应商进行欺诈筛查、信贷决策等核心业务，单一供应商被攻破可能导致风险沿运营、信息和金融链路传播，引发类似传统银行危机的损失。

Method: 构建包含AI供应商、金融机构、同业敞口和客户账户的四层异构网络；提出CFC-Prop随机流行病与清算模型模拟风险传播；训练CFC-GNN早期预警模型利用遥测数据和图结构识别高风险供应商。

Result: 在合成数据集上，CFC-Prop重现了重尾损失分布和对补丁延迟的敏感性；CFC-GNAI在AUROC 0.82和AUPRC 0.60的表现优于四个基线模型，且校准误差可控。

Conclusion: AI供应商的网络集中度是首要的金融稳定性问题，本文为监管机构提供了量化推理工具，并公开了代码、数据和复现脚本。

Abstract: The banking system now depends on a small set of shared artificial intelligence vendors for fraud screening, credit decisioning, anti-money-laundering triage, customer analytics, and internal decision support. This paper studies how a compromise inside one of those vendors can propagate along a chain of operational, informational, and financial linkages until it triggers losses that look, from the outside, like a classical banking crisis. We build a four-layer heterogeneous network that couples AI vendors, financial institutions, interbank exposures, and customer accounts, and we propose CFC-Prop, a stochastic epidemic-and-clearing model that runs on that network. On a synthetic dataset with 60 vendors, 220 banks, roughly 2,500 vendor-bank service edges, and 1,400 interbank exposures, CFC-Prop reproduces the heavy-tailed loss distributions and the sharp dependence on patch latency that are consistent with prior cyber-financial evidence. We also train an early-warning model, CFC-GNN, that uses vendor-side incident telemetry and graph structure to flag high-cascade-risk vendors before impact. Across four baselines the proposed model reaches AUROC 0.82 and AUPRC 0.60 while keeping calibration errors bounded. We release the full code, synthetic data, and reproducible scripts. The results argue that cyber concentration among AI vendors is a first-order financial-stability problem and give supervisors a concrete quantitative tool for reasoning about it.

</details>


### [199] [Fortunate Recall: Ontology-Driven Memory Lifecycle Management for Persistent Coherence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10413)
*Ansuman Mullick,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fortunate Recall (FR) 是一种可组合的 LLM 记忆策略层，通过基于行为本体的分类实施差异化生命周期管理。FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench 上达到 76.9% 的通过率，显著优于现有方法，并将幻觉率降低约一半，证实了行为本体和特定生命周期策略对提升记忆检索精度和校准的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前大语言模型（LLM）记忆系统中个人事实存储无限增长且检索精度下降的问题。核心挑战在于缺乏针对不同类型事实的生命周期管理（即决定哪些记忆应保留、替换及更新频率）。

Method: 提出 Fortunate Recall (FR)，一种基于 10+1 行为本体的策略层。该方法将个人事实分类，并根据类别应用特定的确定性生命周期策略（包括差异化的时间衰减、槽位键覆盖、事件时间有效性和类别感知的检索路由）。实现了基础设施无关的 FR-Bank 系统，并构建了新的基准测试 LifecycleBench 进行验证。

Result: FR-Bank 在 LifecycleBench 上获得 76.9% 的通过率，领先于 Mem0、A-MEM 等基线模型；在 LongMemEval-S 上达到 75.2% 的正确率。消融实验显示，行为本体显著降低了下游幻觉率（从 24.2% 降至 12.0%），并在 BEAM 基准测试中进一步验证了其优势（46.8% vs 32.9%）。

Conclusion: 基于行为本体的生命周期管理策略能有效提升 LLM 记忆的准确性和可靠性，减少幻觉，且不损害标准检索性能。该研究开源了本体、基准测试及代码，为 LLM 记忆系统的精细化治理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current LLM memory systems treat all personal facts identically, so stores grow without bound while retrieval precision degrades. The core challenge is lifecycle management: which memories should persist, which should be replaced, and at what rate, conditioned on the behavioral type of each fact. Fortunate Recall (FR) is a composable policy layer that classifies personal facts into a 10+1 behavioral ontology and applies category-specific lifecycle policies (differential temporal decay, slot-key supersession, event-time validity, and category-aware retrieval routing) as deterministic functions over LLM-extracted metadata. FR-Bank, our infrastructure-independent implementation, reaches a 76.9% pass rate on LifecycleBench, a new 516-question temporal-disambiguation benchmark, ahead of Mem0, A-MEM, Memory-R1, and MemoryOS (61% to 70.5%), and 75.2% on the full LongMemEval-S under the canonical Wu et al. judge protocol, so lifecycle policies impose no measurable cost on standard retrieval. A pre-registered ablation locates the gains: replacing the typed layer with three generic lifecycle primitives leaves correctness statistically unchanged (-1.7pp, 95% CI [-6.0, +2.7]), so the generic lifecycle metadata carries the correctness advantage, while the behavioral ontology carries calibration, halving downstream confabulation (12.0% vs 24.2%, p<0.001). End-to-end, FR-Bank cuts confabulation from Mem0's 45.1% to 22.4% over answered queries and from 32.2% to 13.0% over all queries while answering more of them correctly (31.2% vs 18.6%); the ranking replicates on the open-weight Kimi K2.5. The decomposition transfers to BEAM, an independently built benchmark: 46.8% correct vs Mem0's 32.9% over 280 questions, with the ontology's benefit concentrated in contradiction resolution and saturating near seven policy clusters. The ontology, benchmark, and code are released.

</details>


### [200] [ConvMem: Convolutional Memory for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10441)
*Hongming Zhang,Zhaozhen Gu,Fengshuo Bai,Ming Hao,Qingyang Zhang,Yuanyuan Wang,Shiyang Tang,Yanna Wang,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ConvMem，一种免训练、高度并行的长上下文推理框架，通过层级卷积机制将线性推理路径转化为对数树结构，显著提升效率并避免过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLMs）受限于固定上下文窗口，而顺序处理方法（如MemAgent）存在高延迟和昂贵的强化学习（RL）训练成本，且易导致特定数据集过拟合。

Method: 将LLM提示特定查询视为卷积核，采用层级卷积方式总结文本片段；结合可配置步幅（Configurable Strides）、跳跃连接（Skip Connections）和多核卷积（Multi-Kernel Convolution），实现证据捕获、传播及复杂查询的语义分解。

Result: 在RULER-HotpotQA和RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA基准测试中，ConvMem性能优于无训练基线方法，且在分布外任务上避免了RL训练模型常见的参数先验过拟合风险。

Conclusion: ConvMem通过并行化和层级处理有效解决了长上下文推理中的延迟和过拟合问题，为高效长上下文理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities, they often struggle with extremely long contexts due to fixed context limits. To address this, sequential approaches like MemAgent extend the effective context by reading text in segments and iteratively updating a fixed-size memory. However, this sequential paradigm suffers from high latency and requires costly reinforcement learning (RL) training, which can lead to overfitting on specific datasets. To overcome these limitations, we propose ConvMem, a training-free, highly parallelizable framework that reformulates long-context reasoning as a hierarchical convolution. Inspired by CNNs, ConvMem treats an LLM prompted with a specific query as a convolutional kernel. This kernel summarizes text segments hierarchically, shortening the reasoning path from a linear chain into a logarithmic tree. Specifically, ConvMem integrates \textit{Configurable Strides} and \textit{Skip Connections} to ensure robust evidence capture and propagation, while employing \textit{Multi-Kernel Convolution} to decompose complex queries into disentangled semantic channels. This design not only mitigates error accumulation but also enables massive parallelization across both text segments and reasoning threads. Experiments on RULER-HotpotQA and RULER-2WikiMultiHopQA demonstrate that ConvMem outperforms training-free baselines and avoids the risk of overfitting to parametric priors often observed in RL-trained models on out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [201] [JarvisGUI: Towards Cross-Device GUI Agents with Dynamic Task Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10451)
*Zixiang Chen,Yuheng Lu,Zihao Cheng,Zeming Liu,Jizeng Bai,Ziye Huang,Zhiyin Lin,Zihan Li,Yuhang Guo,Yunhong Wang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出JarvisGUI，这是一个动态基准测试，用于评估GUI智能体在跨设备（Android、Windows、Ubuntu）工作流中的能力，揭示了现有基准测试因仅关注单设备任务而高估了智能体的现实适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的GUI使用涉及跨设备和平台的复杂工作流，需要中间结果传输、共享状态维护和异构环境协调。然而，现有的GUI基准测试主要评估单设备、静态定义的任务，导致对智能体在真实场景下能力的评估过于乐观，且缺乏对跨设备能力的考察。

Method: 引入JarvisGUI基准测试，将GUI任务建模为轻量级类型系统下的输入-输出转换。该方法允许自动组合多步骤、跨设备的工作流，并在统一的框架内动态评估智能体性能。通过在涵盖多个操作系统的虚拟环境中进行评估来实现。

Result: 评估结果显示，最先进的开源GUI智能体在处理跨设备工作流所需的状态转移意识、跨平台上下文推理和长程依赖管理方面存在困难。这暴露了现有基准测试未能发现的智能体关键能力差距。

Conclusion: JarvisGUI填补了跨设备GUI智能体评估的空白，证明了现有基准测试的局限性，并指出了当前智能体在实现真正现实世界应用方面仍存在的显著不足。

Abstract: Real-world GUI usage frequently involves workflows that span multiple devices and platforms, requiring the transfer of intermediate results, maintenance of shared state, and coordination across heterogeneous environments. However, existing GUI benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate agents on single-device, statically defined tasks, thus leaving such cross-device capabilities largely unexamined, resulting in an overly optimistic assessment of agents' readiness for real-world usage. We introduce JarvisGUI, a dynamic benchmark that evaluates GUI agents on cross-device workflows requiring coordinated interaction across heterogeneous platforms, including Android, Windows, and Ubuntu. Specifically, JarvisGUI formulates GUI tasks as input-output transformations under a lightweight type system, which allows us to automatically compose multi-step, cross-device workflows and dynamically evaluate agent performance within a unified framework. By evaluating agents in virtual environments spanning multiple operating systems, JarvisGUI reveals that state-of-the-art open-source GUI agents struggle with the state-transfer awareness, cross-platform contextual reasoning, and long-horizon dependency management required for real-world workflows, exposing a critical capability gap invisible to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [202] [Less Is Personal: Learning Minimal Sufficient User Profiles for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08180)
*Minghang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ENOUGH方法，通过迭代添加行为记录或停止来构建自适应长度的最小充分用户画像，以在保持个性化效果的同时减少上下文成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化语言模型通常固定 prepend 检索到的用户记录数量，即使这些记录是冗余、有害或与用户独特行为无关的，导致效率低下和潜在噪声干扰。

Method: 引入ENOUGH方法，采用离线有界反事实搜索评估画像前缀（综合考虑下游增益、用户特异性和token成本），并将长视域目标蒸馏为具有显式排序和停止监督的多头价值控制器。推理时，控制器通过轻量级决策选择并排序记录，冻结生成器仅在停止后被调用一次。

Result: 在六个个性化任务上的广泛实验表明，ENOUGH在有效性和效率上均一致优于强大的启发式和检索增强基线，实现了保留个性化效用同时降低不必要上下文成本的“最小充分”画像。

Conclusion: ENOUGH提供了一种高效且有效的个性化语言模型构建方式，通过动态调整输入长度来平衡个性化效用与计算成本，证明了最小充分个人化策略的优势。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented personalization enables large language models to produce more accurate and preference-aligned outputs using relevant records retrieved from user histories. Personalized language models typically prepend a fixed number of retrieved user records, even when additional history is redundant, harmful, or unrelated to a user's distinctive behavior. We study minimal sufficient personalization: constructing the least costly ordered profile for each input while preserving the utility achievable from a retrieved candidate pool. We introduce ENOUGH, a method that iteratively appends behavioral records or emits STOP to construct profiles with adaptive lengths. Offline, bounded counterfactual search evaluates profile prefixes by jointly considering downstream gains, user specificity, and token costs. The resulting long-horizon targets are distilled into a multi-head value controller with explicit ranking and stopping supervision. At inference, the controller selects and orders records through lightweight decisions, and the frozen generator is invoked once after stopping. Extensive experiments on six personalized tasks demonstrate that ENOUGH consistently outperforms strong heuristic and retrieval-augmented baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, achieving minimal sufficient profiles that preserve personalization utility while reducing unnecessary context costs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Extracting Semantics from Cattle Reporting Categories for Data Interoperability and Findability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09381)
*Kassy Raymond,Andrew Hamilton-Wright,Deborah Stacey*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种自下而上的方法，通过分析数据集中现有术语的构成和语义（以牛数据为例），在不依赖元数据生成或强制遵循现有标准词汇表的情况下，提高牲畜数据的互操作性和可发现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球健康模型需要按年龄、性别和生产类型细分的家畜人口数据，但现有数据分散且缺乏统一标准。传统通过索引标准化元数据来提高数据可发现性的方法耗时耗力，且在缺乏广泛适用标准的领域（如畜牧业）难以实施；同时，现有的元数据通常需要与预定义的词汇表进行“交叉映射”，成本高昂。

Method: 采用自下而上的方法，利用真实世界的数据集报告类别，提取并分析数据中已存在术语的构成和语义。以牛数据为试点，分析了来自四个数据源的五个数据集中的术语。

Result: 研究发现，现有数据中的年龄、性别和生产修饰符所捕捉到的粒度比全球最大的农业词汇表AGROVOC更细致。这些术语的构成和语义可用于改进数据的互操作性和可发现性，无需先生成元数据或创建标准。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了强制数据集适应现有词汇表，而是利用数据集中已存在的术语嵌入语义，使系统能够在保持文化和数据集特定术语的同时，提高数据的可发现性和互操作性。

Abstract: Livestock population data disaggregated by age, sex, and production are important inputs to calculations and models that inform our understanding of global health, yet these data are fragmented across disparate sources. Bridging data siloes to improve the findability of data requires interoperability. Conventional approaches to improving the findability and interoperability of data include indexing standardized metadata. However, creating metadata is time and resource-intensive and is often difficult in domains such as livestock, which lack standards that address the needs of broad user groups. When metadata exist, they typically need to be standardized against a pre-existing vocabulary, ontology, or thesaurus, requiring a technique known as `crosswalking'. To overcome issues in the absence of metadata, the lack of standards, and the resource-intensive solutions that currently exist, this study uses a bottom-up approach. By leveraging real-world reporting categories in datasets, the composition and semantics of terms already present in the data were extracted and analyzed. Using cattle data as a pilot, we find the age, sex, and production modifiers present across cattle terms from five datasets from four data sources capture granularity not present in AGROVOC, the largest agricultural vocabulary in the world. We discuss how the composition and semantics of these terms can be used to improve the interoperability and findability of data without first requiring metadata to be generated or standards to be created. Rather than forcing datasets to conform to an existing vocabulary, this approach uses the semantics embedded in terms already present in datasets, allowing systems to make data more discoverable and interoperable while maintaining culturally and dataset-specific terminology.

</details>


### [204] [Should I Be Polite to My LLM Relevance Judge? Tone as a Severity Operating-Point Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09703)
*Tian Zhang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 大语言模型作为相关性评判者时，提示语气（tone）会显著影响其评分一致性。研究发现，语气变化主要导致模型整体评分宽松度（severity operating point）的偏移，而非判断能力的提升。这种效应在不同模型间差异巨大，且对基于校准的一致性影响大于对排序指标（如NDCG@10）的影响。语气被视为绝对相关性标签场景下的潜在有效性威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型被广泛用作相关性评判者，但其输出标签随提示表面形式（如语气）的变化而波动，这引发了对其稳定性和有效性的担忧。本研究旨在深入探究“语气”这一特定特征对模型评判行为的具体影响机制。

Method: 研究使用了3,498个TREC DL19/DL20查询-段落对，涵盖8种评判模型、5种分类器校准的礼貌等级以及每级3种改写文本。通过对比不同语气下模型的评分与人类标注的一致性，分析语气变化对模型评分分布和排序结果的影响。

Result: 1. 效应具有强烈的模型依赖性：部分模型呈现结构化U型响应，多数仅显示微小变化。
2. 机制解释：语气变化引起的一致性改变主要源于模型整体评分宽松度的偏移（即向或远离人类标注者的严格程度移动），而非判断质量的改善。跨查询交叉拟合验证了这一关联（Spearman ρ= -0.683; p = 0.019）。
3. 指标影响差异：语气对基于校准的一致性影响大于对排序结果的影响。在32个模型-语气对比中，NDCG@10的最大平均绝对变化仅为0.011，但Kendall's τ低至0.743表明重排序现象依然存在但未完全消除。

Conclusion: 该研究调和了先前矛盾的发现，明确指出提示语气是影响大语言模型作为相关性评判者时绝对相关性标签有效性的潜在威胁因素。在实际应用中，需警惕语气变化导致的评分偏差，特别是在依赖绝对相关性标签的场景中。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as relevance judges, yet their labels can shift with prompt surface form. We study one such feature -- tone -- on 3,498 TREC DL19/DL20 query-passage pairs, across eight judge models, five classifier-calibrated politeness levels, and three paraphrases per level. Effects are strongly model-dependent: one judge shows a structured U-shaped response, whereas most show only small changes. Where tone changes agreement, the results are more consistent with a shift in the judge's severity operating point -- its overall scoring leniency -- than with improved judgment. Agreement rises or falls as this shift moves the judge toward or away from human annotators' strictness. A query-disjoint cross-fit retains the expected association (Spearman $ρ= -0.683$; exact model-block permutation $p = 0.019$). Tone affects calibration-based agreement more than ranking outcomes: across 32 model-tone contrasts, the largest absolute mean change in NDCG@10 is 0.011, although Kendall's $τ$ as low as 0.743 shows that reordering is reduced, not absent. The account reconciles prior contradictory findings and identifies prompt tone as a potential validity threat when absolute relevance labels matter.

</details>


### [205] [Purchase Advice and Observable Buyer Responses in Real AI Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09878)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究审计了317条AI助手对话记录，发现虽然68条记录包含购买导向内容，但没有任何记录显示用户最终完成购买或明确放弃购买。核心结论是：现有的对话日志无法准确衡量AI助手的实际说服率或转化率，因为可观察到的推荐内容远多于可观察到的后续决策，存在显著的数据测量局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决生成式AI助手在商业场景中“说服”用户购买的频率难以量化问题。由于对话日志通常只记录推荐行为而缺乏后续的购买决策数据，导致无法准确评估AI的实际转化效果。

Method: 对Aiso专有数据库中317条经过许可、同意且去标识的历史交互记录进行审计。通过单智能体AI辅助筛选，识别出67条保留的购买相关片段（2023年4月至2025年7月）。研究采用文本无注释的方式，对助手提供的选项、渠道及用户后续互动进行描述性统计分析。

Result: 1. 在67条保留片段中，52条（77.6%）包含候选选项、获取渠道或条件偏好；仅1条涉及从特定住宿候选人处转移。
2. 没有片段被编码为建议放弃或推迟购买类别。
3. 仅有18条（26.9%）片段在同一购买任务中包含后续用户轮次，这表明仅凭对话深度会高估后续可用性27.8%。
4. 在47条用户后续消息中，未观察到任何明确的购买承诺、已完成购买报告或购买类别放弃声明。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心发现是一种测量局限性：推荐内容的可观察性远高于买家后续决策的可观察性。所选样本、未经验证的AI注释以及缺失的交易结果不支持对说服力进行总体水平或因果估计。研究提供了操作定义和可复现的描述性结果，强调了当前数据在衡量AI转化效果上的不足。

Abstract: How often does a generative assistant persuade someone to buy, or persuade them not to buy? Conversation logs contain recommendations, but they do not necessarily record subsequent decisions. We audit 317 historical interactions from Aiso's proprietary research database of licensed, consent-based, de-identified conversations with commercially available AI assistants. Single-agent AI-assisted screening identifies 68 purchase-directed records; collapsing one shared-prefix copy yields 67 retained episodes, dated April 2023 to July 2025. Assistant responses provide candidate options, acquisition channels, or conditional preferences in 52 episodes (77.6%). One episode contains conditional redirection away from a named accommodation candidate. No episode is coded as advice to abandon or defer the purchase category. Only 18 episodes (26.9%) contain a subsequent user turn within the same purchase-related mission, compared with 23 (34.3%) that contain any later user turn. Using conversation depth alone therefore overstates this follow-up availability by 27.8%. Across 47 retained user follow-up messages, no explicit post-advice purchase commitment, completed-purchase report, or purchase-category abandonment statement is observed. These zeros describe recorded statements, not conversion or persuasion rates. The paper supplies operational definitions, text-free annotations, and reproducible descriptive results. Its central finding is a measurement limitation: recommendation content is observable much more often than a buyer's subsequent decision. The selected historical sample, unvalidated AI annotations, and missing transaction outcomes do not support a population-level or causal estimate of persuasion.

</details>


### [206] [Guaranteeing Faithful Evidence Extraction in Speculative Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10046)
*Quentin Signé,Mohand Boughanem,Jose Moreno,Thiziri Belkacem*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CHyD（Constrained Hybrid Decoding）的新范式，用于推测性RAG，通过强制解码约束确保提取的引用片段与检索文档完全一致，解决了现有方法在安全关键领域无法保证字面忠实度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在信息检索中易产生幻觉和忠实度错误，即生成答案与检索证据不符。现有的检索增强生成（RAG）及混合/半提取方法虽有所缓解，但不能保证引文或提取片段是检索上下文的逐字原文，这在需要严格匹配认证文档的安全关键领域可能导致严重后果。

Method: 引入CHyD，将传统用于优化推理速度的推测解码架构重新用于确保忠实度。该方法实施了硬解码约束，限制生成过程仅限于检索文档中存在的连续片段，从而提供明确保证：输出中明确引用的任何片段均在提供的上下文中逐字出现。

Result: 在多种最先进的LLM上进行的评估显示，现有混合方法在技术领域中经常 hallucinate 引文片段，精确提取准确率低于40%。相比之下，CHyD实现了近乎完美的提取忠实度。尽管硬约束引入了与流畅性指标的权衡，但该方法提高了确切答案的正确率，并在整体表现上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CHyD为安全关键领域的信息检索应用提供了可靠的解决方案，能够在保证严格字面忠实度的同时，维持具有竞争力的答案正确性和整体性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interfaces for information retrieval, but they remain prone to hallucinations and faithfulness errors, in which the generated answers diverge from the retrieved evidence. While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and recent hybrid or semi-extractive approaches mitigate this issue, they do not guarantee that quoted or extracted spans are verbatim from the retrieved context. This limitation can have severe consequences in safety-critical domains, where answers must exactly match certified documentation.
  We introduce Constrained Hybrid Decoding (CHyD), a novel faithfulness-first paradigm for speculative RAG. While traditional speculative decoding is optimized for inference speed, CHyD repurposes this architecture to ensure faithful verbatim evidence extraction when the extraction mode is correctly triggered. Our approach enforces hard decoding constraints that restrict generation to continuous spans present in the retrieved documents. This design provides a robust but straightforward guarantee: any explicitly quoted span in the output appears verbatim in the provided context.
  We evaluate our method across state-of-the-art LLMs on diverse abstractive, extractive, and semi-extractive QA benchmarks, including technical datasets motivated by aircraft maintenance. Results show that existing hybrid methods frequently hallucinate quoted spans, with exact extraction accuracy dropping below 40% in technical domains. In contrast, our approach achieves near-perfect extraction faithfulness regardless of the model used. Although enforcing hard constraints introduces a trade-off with fluency-oriented metrics, our method improves exact answer correctness and remains competitive overall, highlighting its suitability for safety-critical information retrieval applications.

</details>


### [207] [LiteRAG: Cost-Efficient Graph-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10239)
*Daniel Alejandro Coll Tejeda,Pedro García López,Daniel Barcelona-Pons*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LiteRAG，一种基于图的检索方法，通过算法探索和推理链构建替代昂贵的LLM控制，显著降低查询延迟和成本，同时保持高质量的多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的检索方法在多跳问答中存在查询时间成本高、生成的上下文过大且分散，从而降低生成效率的问题。

Method: LiteRAG采用查询条件的算法探索和推理链上下文构建，取代了昂贵的检索时LLM控制。关键机制包括查询自适应阈值和社区感知中心节点惩罚。

Result: 在DistComp基准上，LiteRAG获得最高整体质量（0.798），相比GraphRAG Global和DRIFT，查询延迟减少100倍以上，成本减少99%以上。在UltraDomain基准上，其整体质量与LinearRAG相当，但使用的token数减少约14倍。消融研究表明，查询自适应阈值和社区感知中心节点惩罚是提升token效率的主要因素。

Conclusion: LiteRAG通过创新的检索策略，在保证多跳问答质量的同时，大幅提升了检索效率和降低了计算资源消耗，为高效图基检索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval can improve multi-hop question answering, but existing approaches often incur high query-time costs and produce diffuse, oversized contexts that reduce generation efficiency. We present LiteRAG, a graph-based retrieval method that replaces expensive retrieval-time LLM control with query-conditioned algorithmic exploration and reasoning-chain context construction. On DistComp, a benchmark for multi-hop retrieval over distributed-systems papers, LiteRAG attains the highest overall quality among the evaluated methods (0.798) while reducing per-query latency by over 100$\times$ and cost by over 99% relative to GraphRAG Global and DRIFT. On UltraDomain, it matches LinearRAG on overall quality while using about 14$\times$ fewer tokens. An ablation study indicates that LiteRAG's query-adaptive thresholding and community-aware hub penalization are the main drivers of its token-efficiency gains.

</details>
